[@BrandJet AI](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/brandjet-ai/)



Marsad here, founder of brandjet.

After years in GTM, including work with Fortune 500 teams, I kept seeing the same problem: every startup and agency was juggling a stack of tools just to run outreach. Email in one place, LinkedIn in another, WhatsApp and Instagram somewhere else, plus deliverability tools, analytics, and CRMs. It was chaos.

Brand intelligence and outreach never spoke to each other.

So we built BrandJet, a single command centre for both.

All your outreach channels live in one platform: email, LinkedIn, WhatsApp, and Instagram. You get shared and channel-level analytics, along with an AI unibox that handles sentiment and lead categorization.

What I’m most proud of is our monitoring tool. It lets you track sentiment, mentions, keywords, and even how AI systems interpret your brand. Any topic, any company, and everything feeds directly into your outreach automations.

No guesswork. No hassle. Just clear signals and fast action.

If you’re tired of SaaS bloat and want to see what a unified GTM platform feels like, give it a try. I’d love to hear what you think

[29d ago](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/vidocu/comment/578638/)

[@Vidocu](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/vidocu/)

Buckle up- this is a long one- but worth the read, and DESERVES THIS LEVEL OF HYPE!

[@Vidocu](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/vidocu/) is, without exaggeration, the most impressive AI video platform I have used, and I have tested nearly everything in this space.

VEED. Descript. Camtasia. Powtoon. D-ID. Vyond. Microsoft tools. Google tools. GitHub MCP workflows. AI automation stacks. Screen recording suites. Video editors. AI presenters. Knowledge-to-video tools. The list honestly goes on for another couple dozen platforms.

Nothing comes close to what Vidocu is doing right now.

What makes this platform different is not just the output quality. It is the fact that it solves the actual operational problem companies have.

Most startups and scaling organizations already have a goldmine of content sitting around:
• Product demos
• Sales enablement recordings
• Deep dive walkthroughs
• Internal training sessions
• Support calls
• Feature explanations
• Webinar recordings
• Loom videos nobody watches twice

Normally, turning those into polished customer-facing assets is painful. It takes editing skills, scripting, voiceovers, formatting, branding, music selection, timing adjustments, article writing, distribution prep, and hours upon hours of cleanup.

Vidocu turns that entire nightmare into:
Drag. Drop. Done.

And I do not mean “AI done” in the usual disappointing sense where you still spend 3 hours fixing everything manually afterward.

I mean genuinely customer-ready.

Within minutes, Vidocu transforms raw recordings into polished, brand-aligned video content that looks like a professional media team produced it.

The AI voiceover quality is honestly absurd. Natural pacing. Human sounding delivery. Proper synchronization to the video. Intelligent script cleanup. Smooth narration flow. Subtle professional background music. Intelligent scene transitions. Intro and outro generation. It all feels cohesive instead of stitched together.

Then it gets even more ridiculous.

One more click, and suddenly that same recording becomes a polished, customer-facing article. Structured. Clean. Branded. Readable. Actually useful.

This is where the platform becomes a serious business multiplier.

You are no longer just creating videos. You are instantly building:
• A customer education library
• A support knowledge base
• Product onboarding content
• Internal enablement assets
• Training documentation
• Release walkthroughs
• Self-service customer resources
• Marketing collateral

All from content your company already has sitting around unused.

That is the part people are missing.

Vidocu is not just a video editor. It is a content transformation engine.

The market is unbelievably saturated right now with AI video tools that all promise the same thing. Most of them are incremental improvements on old workflows. Fancy wrappers around complicated editing processes.

Vidocu feels like one of the first tools that actually rethinks the workflow entirely.

And somehow, despite how strong this platform is, it still feels massively undiscovered.

Pricing is more than reasonable for what is being delivered here. Frankly, compared to the amount of time, production effort, and staffing this can replace or accelerate, the value proposition is a no-brainer.

I am currently building a business case to push for full-scale adoption internally because this has the potential to completely change how companies handle customer education, onboarding, support content, and enablement at scale.

I rarely say this about software anymore because most tools are overhyped.

Vidocu is underhyped.

If you create customer-facing content in any capacity, especially within startups, SaaS, support organizations, onboarding teams, customer success, enablement, or product marketing, you need to try this platform.

There is genuinely nothing even close to it right now in the AI video market.

Giddy up!

[1mo ago](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/ozigi/comment/572269/)

[@Ozigi](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/ozigi/)

Hi everyone. 👋
I created [@Ozigi](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/ozigi/) because I was tired of seeing AI slop in content all over the internet, from social media posts to long-form content.
Ozigi operates on two principles:
1\. We eliminate AI slop words from your content, using a banned lexicon feature that filters common/known AI words or structures from getting into your content.
2\. We encourage a human-in-the-loop philosophy. We do not believe in mindless automation that values quantity over quality. We value quality, and that is why it is important to us to make sure that you--a human--reviews the generated content first before setting it up for publishing.

Ozigi does more than just social media content. It generates email newsletters and blogppsts as well.

[![abbey profile picture](https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/a/ACg8ocIs1hWohv1GzwN7PhOTpmWz8DO1GxvqrnYeehOpQgzNN05VENE=s96-c?width=52)\\

🙏 3 karma](https://theresanaiforthat.com/@abbey-5kfds/)

[4mo ago](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/temvideo-ai-video-ad-maker/comment/499629/)

[@TemVideo AI Video Ad Maker](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/temvideo-ai-video-ad-maker/)

I was skeptical about the "one-click video" claim, but I gave TemVideo a shot for a new product I'm testing. The biggest headache with AI video tools is usually the prompt engineering—you spend 30 minutes typing descriptions just to get a weird-looking hand. TemVideo completely bypasses that.

My honest take after using it:

The "From Raw Footage" feature is the real MVP. You know those random 10-second clips you take on your phone just to check lighting? The AI somehow turns those messy scraps into coherent ads. It’s almost spooky how well it edits around the product.

It’s built for speed. If you are a dropshipper who needs to test 5 different products today, this workflow is unbeatable. Upload, click, done. I had an export ready faster than I could find a coffee.

Multi-language is a bonus. It’s smart they included localization. If you sell globally, having the AI handle the lip sync and narration in different languages from one workflow is a massive time-saver.

Best part? The built-in "Viral Hooks" library. It takes the guesswork out of what text to put on screen to stop the scroll.
